#### Section 1: Architecture review

Current scope: mode SELECTIVE EXPANSION (`/autoplan` override). Accepted: C1 to C3, C10 to C27, C29 to C58 except the declined ones. Declined: C4 to C9. Deferred: the metadata refresh feature (C48, to TODOS.md). Pending: C28 (Eng review; a default is in the plan).

```
BEFORE                                              AFTER
┌──────────────┐   ┌───────────────────┐            ┌──────────────┐
│ /board/[id]  │   │ /maintenance      │            │ /board/[id]  │
│ BoardPage    │   │ MaintenanceClient │            │ BoardPage    │
│  Client      │   │ + 4 hooks         │            │  Client      │
└──────┬───────┘   └────────┬──────────┘            └──────┬───────┘
       │ OverflowMenu<TId>  │ Restore / Delete             │ OverflowMenu (RepoCardId)
       │ NoteModal<TId>     │ dialogs                      │ NoteModal
┌──────┴────────────────────┴──────────┐            ┌──────┴────────────────┐
│ repo-cards.ts   project-info.ts      │            │ repo-cards.ts         │
│ moveToMaintenance  maintenance-      │            │ project-info.ts       │
│ restoreToBoard     project-info.ts   │            │   └ project-info-core │
│        └── shared-project-info (FkConfig) ┘       └──────┬────────────────┘
└──────┬────────────────────┬──────────┘                   │
┌──────┴──────┐      ┌──────┴───────┐               ┌──────┴──────┐
│ repocard    │◄─ ─ ─│ maintenance  │  app-only     │ repocard    │
│  └projectinfo (repo_card_id XOR maintenance_id)   │  └projectinfo (repo_card_id NOT NULL)
└─────────────┘      └──────────────┘  exclusivity  └─────────────┘
 RPC: move_to_maintenance, restore_to_board          RPC: move_card_to_board only
```

- Boundaries: one module per concern is left. `project-info-core.ts` holds the non-action code, `project-info.ts` the `'use server'` boundary. No new component, service or table.
- Data flow of the one new flow, the migration:

```
maintenance rows ──► guard ──► per user: board ─► column ─► cards ──► re-point projectinfo ──► drops ──► NOTICE
   │ none (fresh DB)    │ overlap or case variant > 0                         │ no projectinfo rows
   ▼                    ▼                                                     ▼
 loop runs 0 times,   EXCEPTION with two counts,                          UPDATE touches 0 rows
 drops still run      nothing changed                                     (fine)
   error anywhere (lock_timeout, constraint) ──► whole DO block rolls back, job red
```

- State: the card lifecycle loses a state. Before: `on a board ⇄ in Maintenance → deleted`. After: `on a board → deleted`. The transition that needed app-side guarding (restore while the repository was added elsewhere) no longer exists.
- Coupling: removed, not added. `projectinfo` depends on `repocard` only.
- Scale: the migration touches tens of rows. The app loses one query per board load.
- Single point of failure: the production migration job. Handled: one transaction, named re-run and fix-forward paths.
- Security architecture: two RPCs and seven server actions disappear. The two `projectinfo` policies are recreated with the ownership path through `repocard → board`; see Section 3.
- Production failure scenario: the job fails on `lock_timeout` because a board read holds a lock. Handled: re-run through `workflow_dispatch`.
- Rollback: code by `git revert` (the reverted build cannot work, because the table is gone, so rollback of code after a successful migration is not an option; fix forward). Data: rows from the dump, PITR last. Stated in the plan.
- Value test for artifacts about the work: the word-ban test and the scenario script were cut (C1, C2, C56). What remains are the `NOTICE` (consumed in rollout step 7) and the PR-body record of the rehearsal (consumed by the reviewer of the PR and by anyone who has to explain the migration later; smallest useful form: counts; it retires with the PR).

Findings: none beyond the ledger. The architecture gets simpler in every dimension it changes.

#### Section 2: Error & rescue map

```
CODEPATH                               | WHAT CAN GO WRONG                      | ERROR
---------------------------------------|----------------------------------------|---------------------------
migration guard                        | overlap or case-variant entries        | plpgsql EXCEPTION (P0001)
migration locks                        | another session holds a lock > 5 s     | lock_not_available (55P03)
migration card insert                  | unique or trigger violation            | 23505 / check_violation
migration policy loop                  | policy count is not 2 (drift)          | none, WARNING
production migration job               | any of the above, or CLI failure       | job red
meta fill (rollout step 10)            | gh api 404 or renamed repository       | per repository, skipped
                                       | SQL error inside the DO block          | rolled back, trigger state unchanged
moveCardToBoard (unchanged)            | target holds the repository (23505)    | ActionUserError, shown verbatim
getUserRepoPlacements (one query now)  | query fails                            | Error, picker shows its error state
getUserBoardsWithStatusLists (dialog)  | query fails                            | dialog shows boardsError
account page counts                    | a count query fails                    | none, count shows 0

ERROR                        | RESCUED? | RESCUE ACTION                                   | USER SEES
-----------------------------|----------|-------------------------------------------------|---------------------------
guard EXCEPTION              | Y        | nothing changed; remove the overlap, re-run     | nothing (job log only)
lock_not_available           | Y        | re-run through workflow_dispatch                | nothing
23505 / check in migration   | Y        | rolled back; fix the same file, new commit      | nothing
policy count WARNING         | Y        | policies are dropped by loop and recreated      | nothing
gh api 404                   | Y        | card keeps empty meta                           | bare title on that card
moveCardToBoard 23505        | Y        | existing ActionUserError path                   | inline error in the dialog
placements query failure     | Y        | existing error state and retry in the picker    | "could not load" + retry
account count failure        | N ← pre-existing | count falls back to 0                   | a wrong 0 ← not introduced here
```

One pre-existing gap: `/account` shows 0 when a count query fails (`account/page.tsx:58`, `?? 0`). The plan touches this file but not this behavior. Not a silent failure the plan introduces; recorded in the failure registry as pre-existing, no task.

#### Section 3: Security & threat model

- Attack surface: shrinks. Removed: `move_to_maintenance`, `restore_to_board` (both `SECURITY INVOKER`), `moveToMaintenance`, `restoreToBoard`, five maintenance project-info actions, the `/maintenance` route. Added: one static link.
- Authorization: the recreated `projectinfo` policies are the only security-relevant code this plan writes. Threat: a policy recreated too wide exposes notes of other users. Likelihood low, impact high. Mitigation in the plan: same names and same `(select auth.uid())` form, `WITH CHECK` equal to `USING`, and the rehearsal checks that two policies exist. **Added check (C59):** in scenario A, read `projectinfo` as the second user through a session with that user's JWT claims (`SET LOCAL role authenticated; SET LOCAL request.jwt.claims`) and expect only that user's rows. The eng review turns this into the exact statements.
- `repo_card_id NOT NULL` removes orphan `projectinfo` rows as a class.
- Input validation: no new input.
- Secrets: none new. The `meta` fill uses the owner's `gh` token locally; nothing is committed.
- Data classification: the dumps hold both users' board data and notes. Mitigation: C58 (private directory, mode 600, not synced), and `pnpm db:reset` after the local check removes the local copy. The screenshots are checked for private repository names (C52).
- Injection: the `meta` fill passes values as one dollar-quoted JSON document, so repository descriptions cannot break out of the statement, provided the dollar-quote tag does not occur in the data. **Added (C60):** the tag is random per run and the script refuses to send if the JSON contains it.
- Audit logging: no auth lifecycle event changes.

#### Section 4: Data flow & interaction edge cases

Migration flow with shadow paths is drawn in Section 1. Node by node: guard (tested in scenario A, both counts), board/column/cards (A and B), re-point (A and B, `updated_at` compared), drops (B: verification query), `NOTICE` (A and B).

Async ordering. Invariant: no write to `maintenance`, `projectinfo` or `repocard` lands between the guard and the drops.

```
old build action            migration job                    shared state
----------------            -------------                    ------------
                            LOCK (ACCESS EXCLUSIVE) ───────► tables locked
moveToMaintenance ──► waits on the lock
                            guard, move, re-point, drops
                            COMMIT ────────────────────────► table and RPC gone
resumes: RPC missing ──► error toast, card stays on the board
```

The other order (the old action commits first) happens before the lock; the migration then sees one more entry and moves it. An overlap cannot be created by the old build (it checks both places). The new build is not live before the merge, except on previews (C31). The lock is the mechanism; no further test is needed beyond scenario A.

| Interaction                      | Edge case                                      | Handled?       | How                                                                                                           |
| -------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------- | -------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Move dialog, no other board      | click "Create a board" with the dialog open    | yes            | plain navigation to `/boards/new`; the dialog unmounts with the page                                          |
| Move dialog                      | double-click Move                              | yes, unchanged | the button is disabled while pending                                                                          |
| Picker with placements unknown   | select a repository that sits on another board | yes            | the server refuses with the board's name (`ActionUserError`); only the page-load Maintenance fallback is gone |
| Board "Maintenance"              | 23 or more cards in one column                 | yes            | an ordinary column; the user can add columns and drag                                                         |
| `/maintenance` bookmark          | open it after the deploy                       | yes            | `not-found.tsx`                                                                                               |
| Old tab                          | Move to Maintenance after the migration        | yes            | error toast (action of the old build fails), reload brings the new client                                     |
| Persisted note draft of an entry | open the migrated card                         | yes            | same id, the draft continues                                                                                  |

No unhandled edge case.

#### Section 5: Code quality

- Fit: the plan follows the existing split (pure helpers outside `'use server'` files, `ActionResult`, `withAuth*` guards). No new pattern.
- Duplication: none added. Removed: the maintenance copies of five project-info actions and two dialogs.
- Naming: `project-info-core.ts` says what the file is once "shared" is false. `BoardPlacement[]` replaces a wrapper with one key.
- Over-engineering removed: `FkConfig`, generic `TId`, `CardIdentifier`, the pick-and-skip branch of the migration.
- Under-engineering: none found.
- Open: C28 (`useOptimisticCardAction` with one caller), owned by the eng review.

No issues beyond C28.

#### Section 6: Test review

```
NEW OR CHANGED THING                         | TEST                        | TYPE   | IN PLAN
---------------------------------------------|-----------------------------|--------|--------
migration: move, re-point, drops             | rehearsal scenario B        | manual | yes
migration: guard (overlap, letter case)      | rehearsal scenario A        | manual | yes
migration: user with no board                | rehearsal scenario A        | manual | yes
migration: second run is a no-op             | rehearsal step 9            | manual | yes
projectinfo policies after recreation        | scenario A, per-user read   | manual | added (C59)
move dialog empty state links to /boards/new | unit                        | unit   | yes (C55)
/account shows two tiles                     | unit                        | unit   | yes
placement split, 23505 mapping, boards only  | unit (existing, narrowed)   | unit   | yes
park and bring back keeps the note           | e2e, there and back         | e2e    | yes
picker "Already placed elsewhere"            | e2e (existing, other board) | e2e    | yes
landing shows the new fourth card            | e2e hydration list          | e2e    | yes
command palette filter still discriminates   | e2e (assert on Settings)    | e2e    | yes (C17)
meta fill                                    | local copy first + count    | manual | yes (C41)
```

Assertion check, per behavior:

- Migration moves every entry. Assertion: card count on the new board equals the entry count taken before. Rejects: a card lost or duplicated. `no_claim`: says nothing about concurrent writers (the lock covers that) and nothing about a schema that differs from `20261011030000` (production is checked by the verification query after the run).
- Guard. Assertion: `migration up` exits non-zero and `to_regclass('public.maintenance')` is still set. Rejects: a guard that warns and continues. `no_claim=none beyond protects`.
- Policies. Assertion: user B reads 0 `projectinfo` rows of user A. Rejects: a policy without the ownership join. `no_claim`: local roles and claims stand in for a real session; production RLS is exercised by the production check (the owner sees their notes) but a cross-user read is not attempted there.
- Park and bring back. Assertion: after two moves the card's note text is unchanged. Rejects: a move that drops `projectinfo`. `no_claim`: fixture user and MSW GitHub data; the real-account check covers real payloads.
- Move dialog link. Assertion: with zero other boards a link with `href="/boards/new"` is visible. Rejects: text-only empty state. `no_claim=none beyond protects`.

2am-Friday test: scenario B on the production copy. Hostile QA: the guard scenarios. Chaos: the second run and a `lock_timeout` re-run. Pyramid: unit-heavy, three e2e touches, manual only where the subject is a one-time migration. Flakiness: the there-and-back e2e uses the menu and dialog, no drag; low risk. No LLM or prompt change.

Gaps: 0 after C59.

#### Section 7: Performance

- The board page drops `getUserMaintenanceRepoIdentifiers` (one query per load). The placement lookup goes from two parallel queries to one. `/account` runs two counts instead of three.
- Migration: `ACCESS EXCLUSIVE` on three small tables for milliseconds, 5 s `lock_timeout`. No index is added or needed (`projectinfo.repo_card_id` is already unique).
- A board with 23 or more cards in one column renders like any other full column.

No issues.

#### Section 8: Observability & debuggability

- The migration reports through `NOTICE` and `WARNING`; the job log is the record. The verification query is the after-the-fact check.
- App: nothing new to log. Removed actions take their Sentry contexts with them.
- Three weeks later, "where did my Maintenance entries go?" is answered by the board named `Maintenance`, the CHANGELOG entry and the PR body. Sufficient for a 2-user product.
- Runbook: section 6 of the plan is the runbook for the two failure modes.

Gaps: 0.

#### Section 9: Deployment & rollout

- Migration safety: not backward compatible for the old Maintenance page and its two RPCs, by design. Backward compatible for everything the old board page does (C50).
- Order: merge triggers both; the migration lands first by minutes. The new build works on both schemas; the old build degrades on the new one as listed.
- Feature flag: none; a flag cannot guard a dropped table.
- Rollback: stated (fix forward; rows from the dump; PITR last).
- Environment parity: no staging. The rehearsal on a production copy stands in for it.
- Post-deploy: job green, `NOTICE`, verification query, deployment live, browser check, `meta` fill, its check.
- Risk flagged: previews of the PR run the new picker against production (C31). Covered by the accepted rule.

Risks flagged: 1 (covered).

#### Section 10: Long-term trajectory

- Debt: removed (a table, two RPCs, a route, an abstraction, 4 e2e specs). Added: none in code; two TODO entries describe pre-existing gaps (metadata refresh, repo search).
- Reversibility: 2 of 5. The data move is one-way; bringing the feature back would be new work. The dump keeps the values.
- Path dependency: reusing entry ids as card ids has no downside (uuid) and one upside (drafts).
- The 1-year question: a new engineer finds one storage path and one RLS path. Yes, obvious.
- What comes next: repository search across boards and a metadata refresh, both board features that now serve archived repositories too. Were the right cherry-picks accepted? Yes: the declined ones (redirect, preset, favorite, notice, name-based default) would each have kept a trace of the concept.

#### Section 11: Design & UX (UI scope: yes)

- Information architecture. Sidebar after the change: Boards (All Boards, Favorites), then Settings and Shortcuts. The Maintenance item sat alone between two separators; one separator has to go with it or the sidebar shows an empty band. `/boards` gains one card at the end for the owner. `/account` shows two tiles.
- State coverage:

```
FEATURE                    | LOADING        | EMPTY                    | ERROR            | SUCCESS       | PARTIAL
---------------------------|----------------|--------------------------|------------------|---------------|--------
Move dialog                | existing       | text + link (new)        | existing         | existing      | n/a
/account tiles             | existing       | 0 and 0                  | shows 0 (pre-ex.)| two tiles     | n/a
Board "Maintenance"        | as any board   | n/a (created with cards) | as any board     | 23 cards      | cards without meta until step 10
Landing fourth card        | n/a            | n/a                      | n/a              | new copy      | n/a
```

- Journey. The owner opens the app: no Maintenance item in the sidebar (expected, they asked). `/boards`: a board named "Maintenance" at the end. Open it: one column "Archived", their repositories in the order they knew, comments and notes in place. Nothing to learn.
- AI slop risk: low; no new screen. The landing card is the one place with new copy (T3).
- DESIGN.md: none in the repository.
- Responsive: the account tiles and the landing grid already collapse to one column on small screens; the plan changes column counts, which the design review checks at each breakpoint.
- Accessibility: the new link is a real link with visible text; the dialog keeps focus order.

```
sidebar ──► /boards ──► board "Maintenance" ──► card ──► ⋯ menu ──► Move to Another Board ──► working board
                                  ▲                                         │ no other board
                                  └────────────── moved back ◄──────────────┴──► link ──► /boards/new
```

Issues for the design review: the sidebar separator, the two-tile grid at each breakpoint, the final landing copy, the column name "Archived" inside a board named "Maintenance". `/design-review` runs on the live result after implementation.

#### TODO choices

One proposal, decided: **add** "Refresh a card's GitHub metadata" to TODOS.md (C48). What: an action that re-reads a card's repository from GitHub and updates `meta`. Why: `meta` is a snapshot from the moment a card is added; stars, description and language never change afterwards. Pros: cards stay true; the hand-run fill would not have been needed. Cons: GitHub calls per card, rate limits, a new menu entry or a background job. Context: `addRepositoriesToBoard` is the only writer of `meta` today. Effort: human M, CC S. Priority P3. Depends on: nothing. The TODO text is written with the docs in section 5 of the plan.

Approval readiness: PASS. Checked rows C1 to C60: every approved row cites the `/autoplan` auto-decision authority (the owner's direction of 2026-10-12 and the 6 principles) or a factual correction; declined rows C4 to C9 are out of the plan; C28 is pending with a default and an owner; the one deferral (metadata refresh) has its TODO in section 5 of the plan.

### Phase 2: Design review

Methodology read: `autoplan-design-methodology-l78rJR/methodology.md`, ranges 1-600, 601-1200, 1201-1800, 1801-1977 of 1977 lines (through EOF). Input bound for both voices: `autoplan-design-Qf7C34/design-implementation.md`, sha `d8128bae…a5c8ea`.

#### Step 0: design scope

- Initial rating: **4/10**. The plan is exact about data and vague about what the user sees: it lists what disappears, and it leaves the one flow that replaces the feature (set a repository aside, find it, bring it back) to a link and a sentence. A 10 for this plan names every changed surface, each state of the move dialog, the exact copy, the grid at each width and what a keyboard user meets.
- DESIGN.md: none. The review uses universal principles and the patterns already in the code.
- Existing patterns to reuse: shadcn `Dialog`, `Input`, `Label`, `Button`, `Alert`; the accessible link treatment in `HeldRepositoryList.tsx:78` (underline, focus ring, touch size; plain `text-primary` is not enough on every theme); `sonner` toasts with an action; the empty-state layout of the move dialog (icon, muted text); `NavItem` and the sidebar dividers.
- UI scope: Sidebar, Command Palette, card menu and its Remove confirmation, Move to Another Board dialog, `/account` (page and loading state), landing features section, the migrated board itself, two screenshots.
- Focus areas (0D): all seven dimensions, by the `/autoplan` override.
- Mockups (Step 0.5): `DESIGN_READY`, **not generated**. Auto-decided: the plan adds no screen; every change is a removal or a small edit inside an existing component whose current rendering is the reference; and the mockup flow ends in a comparison board that waits for the owner, who is away. `/design-review` checks the rendered result after implementation instead.

#### Step 0.5: dual voices

**CLAUDE SUBAGENT (design, independent review)**, native, completed, `INPUT: design d8128bae…` matches. Verdict: the UI side is a list of deletions plus unwritten copy. 10 findings:

1. High. Parking a repository becomes a detour for a user with one board: link to `/boards/new`, six domain presets, a redirect to the new empty board, no way back to the card. Create the board inside the dialog.
2. High. Removing the sidebar item leaves two stacked dividers.
3. High. Remove from Board now sits right after the only safe option and its confirmation does not mention that option.
4. Medium. The dialog preselects the first board; a mis-move is silent and has no undo. Start without a selection, add an "Open board" toast action.
5. Medium. The parked repository is harder to find: mark the migrated board as favorite; link held rows to the card.
6. Medium. `moveCardToBoard` puts a card at the end of the column; in a long column it lands below the fold.
7. Medium. Landing card: `LayoutGrid` looks like `Columns3`; "Move Between Boards" names a mechanism. Proposes "Set Work Aside" with `Archive` or `ArrowRightLeft`.
8. Medium. States unspecified: boards-fetch error has no retry; a board without columns is a dead end; picker refusal copy; cards whose repository is gone.
9. Medium. Retake the screenshots from a production build, after the divider fix.
10. Low. `/account` grid classes and tile order; `/maintenance` 404 is fine.

**CODEX SAYS (design, UX challenge)**, outside, `OUTSIDE_STATUS: completed provider=codex host=claude`, verdict: findings, P1. 8 findings:

1. High. The link interrupts the task: `CreateBoardForm.tsx:45` navigates to the new board and carries no source card; the default preset is a development taxonomy. Wants the destination created and selected without leaving the move, and an ordinary one-column option.
2. High. "Find again" rests on desktop find-in-page; the app is a standalone PWA (`manifest.ts:16`). Define the accepted reduced experience and test it on a card near the end of the column.
3. Medium. Recovery decisions for the move dialog: retry on fetch failure, an action for a board without columns, a success message that names the destination and opens it; picker placement failure; the permanent state of cards GitHub no longer returns.
4. Medium. The user has to discover the new hierarchy; the 404 says nothing. Wants a handoff with a direct link and one sentence, and the column named "Repositories", not "Archived".
5. Medium. `/account`: one tile grid for content, another for loading (`account/loading.tsx:30`); specify both and the small-screen layout.
6. Medium. The landing title names a command and the grid dictates the message; proposes "Keep Active Work in Focus"; remove the false click cues (`cursor-pointer`, hover lift) from static blocks.
7. Medium. Accessibility is inherited, not specified: where focus goes when the moved card disappears; focus after creation; visible focus, 44 px targets and contrast for the new control.
8. Low. Exactly one divider between board navigation and utilities; no privileged link to the migrated board.

```
DESIGN OUTSIDE VOICES — LITMUS SCORECARD (landing features section):
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  Check                                    Claude        Codex   Consensus
  ─────────────────────────────────────── ───────────── ─────── ──────────
  1. Brand unmistakable in first screen?   NOT SPEC'D    YES     N/A (one voice)
  2. One strong visual anchor?             NOT SPEC'D    YES     N/A (one voice)
  3. Scannable by headlines only?          NO (title is  NO      CONFIRMED: NO
                                           a mechanism)
  4. Each section has one job?             NOT SPEC'D    YES     N/A (one voice)
  5. Cards actually necessary?             NOT SPEC'D    NO      N/A (one voice)
  6. Motion improves hierarchy?            NOT SPEC'D    NO      N/A (one voice)
  7. Premium without decorative shadows?   NOT SPEC'D    NO      N/A (one voice)
  ─────────────────────────────────────── ───────────── ─────── ──────────
  Hard rejections triggered:               none          none    CONFIRMED: none
═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
```

The native voice was not given the litmus list (its prompt is the independent five-question review), so six cells have one voice only. The one confirmed failure (check 3) is fixed in Pass 4. Checks 5 to 7 describe the existing 2×2 feature grid, which this plan does not redesign.

Agreement between the voices: the one-board journey (both High), the sidebar divider, the move dialog's missing recovery states, the landing title, the screenshots after the sidebar fix, the `/account` grid. Disagreement: findability of the migrated board (native: mark it favorite; Codex: no privileged link, a handoff note instead).

#### Pass 1: Information architecture — 5/10 → 9/10

```
SIDEBAR (after)              /boards                      BOARD "Maintenance"
┌──────────────┐             ┌──────────────────────┐     ┌───────────────────┐
│ Boards     ⌄ │             │ board … board        │     │ Repositories   ⋯  │
│  All Boards  │ ──────────► │ board … Maintenance ◄┼──►  │  card  (comment)  │
│  Favorites   │             │        (last)        │     │  card             │
│ ──────────── │             └──────────────────────┘     │  … (23)           │
│ Settings     │                                          └───────────────────┘
│ Shortcuts    │   card ⋯ menu:  Open on GitHub · Note · Move to Another Board
│ theme        │                 ─────────  Remove from Board (destructive)
└──────────────┘
```

- 1A. Double divider after the item is removed (both voices). **Fixed:** the item and one divider go; the order is written in the plan.
- 1B. Nothing in the navigation points at the migrated board. Native voice: mark it favorite. Codex: no privileged link. **Decided: no favorite, no link** (CEO C7 stands); the final report hands the owner the direct link and one sentence. Taste decision T4.
- 1C. A moved card enters at the end of the column (`move_card_to_board`: `MAX(order) + 1`). **Decided: unchanged.** Every card enters a column at the end (add, move); the toast's "Open board" takes the user there. Taste decision T5.
- Remaining gap to 10: no in-app search across boards (existing TODO).

#### Pass 2: Interaction state coverage — 4/10 → 9/10

```
FEATURE                 | LOADING            | EMPTY                           | ERROR                          | SUCCESS                         | PARTIAL
------------------------|--------------------|---------------------------------|--------------------------------|---------------------------------|---------------------------
Move dialog: boards     | existing text      | create form (name, Create board)| error text + "Try again"       | board and column selects        | board without a column:
                        |                    | → blank board, selected         |                                | preselected                     | message + "Open <board>"
Move dialog: create     | "Creating…",       | n/a                             | error under the field          | new board selected, focus on    | n/a
                        | button disabled    |                                 | (role=alert), name kept        | Move                            |
Move dialog: move       | existing           | n/a                             | existing inline alert,         | toast "Moved to <board>" +      | n/a
                        |                    |                                 | selections kept                | "Open board"                    |
Remove from Board       | existing           | n/a                             | existing toast                 | existing                        | n/a
/account tiles          | 2 skeleton tiles   | 0 and 0                         | a count that fails shows 0     | 2 tiles                         | n/a
                        |                    |                                 | (pre-existing)                 |                                 |
Board "Maintenance"     | as any board       | n/a (created with its cards)    | as any board                   | cards with comments and notes   | cards without description
                        |                    |                                 |                                |                                 | until rollout step 10; a
                        |                    |                                 |                                |                                 | repository GitHub no longer
                        |                    |                                 |                                |                                 | returns keeps a bare title
Picker                  | existing           | existing                        | placements unknown: existing   | existing                        | refused duplicate names its
                        |                    |                                 | error state with retry         |                                 | board (existing copy)
```

- 2A. The one-board empty state was a dead end with a link (both voices, High). **Fixed:** the board is created inside the dialog with one neutral column and becomes the destination. This needs a `blank` preset that `createBoard` accepts and `/boards/new` does not list (its six-card grid stays whole).
- 2B. Fetch failure had no way forward. **Fixed:** "Try again".
- 2C. Destination without a column. **Fixed:** link to that board.
- 2D. Success toast did not say where the card went. **Fixed:** names the board, action opens it.
- 2E. `/account` loading state would still show three tiles (`account/loading.tsx:30`). **Fixed:** two.
- A card whose repository is gone on GitHub: same as any such card today (title, a link that leads to GitHub's 404). Stated, unchanged.

#### Pass 3: User journey — 4/10 → 8/10

```
STEP | USER DOES                                   | USER FEELS                    | PLAN SPECIFIES
-----|---------------------------------------------|-------------------------------|------------------------------------------
1    | Owner opens the app after the rollout       | "Where is Maintenance?"       | handoff note in the final report: link + one sentence
2    | Opens /boards                               | sees a board named            | board at the end of the list, name unchanged
     |                                             | Maintenance: relief           |
3    | Opens it                                    | same repositories, same order,| order by last update, comments and notes kept,
     |                                             | notes intact                  | descriptions after step 10
4    | Later: parks a finished repository          | routine                       | card menu → Move to Another Board → Move → toast with Open board
5    | Looks for an old one                        | mild friction (no search)     | find-in-page on desktop; scrolling in the PWA; stated as accepted
6    | Brings it back                              | routine                       | the same dialog in the other direction
7    | New user with one board parks a repository  | no dead end                   | create the board in the dialog, then Move
8    | Anyone about to Remove from Board           | warned                        | confirmation names the safe alternative
```

- 3A. Step 7 broke the task (both voices). **Fixed** in 2A.
- 3B. Step 8: the destructive action had no pointer to the safe one (native, High). **Fixed:** one sentence in the confirmation.
- 3C. Step 5 on a phone or in the installed app (Codex, High). **Decided: accepted reduction, now stated** in the Goal; the local check walks it at a phone-sized viewport on a card near the end of the column.
- 3D. Step 1: the 404 and the sidebar say nothing (Codex). **Decided:** no in-app notice (CEO C8 stands, the one affected user asked for the change); the handoff note covers it.
- Five-second, five-minute, five-year view: nothing new to learn in the first seconds; the move dialog is the daily tool and now recovers from each failure; over years the archive grows in one column, and search across boards is the known next step.
- Remaining gap to 10: step 5.

#### Pass 4: AI slop risk — 6/10 → 8/10

Classifier: the app surfaces are OPERATE; the landing section is PERSUADE.

- Hard rejections: none (both voices).
- Litmus, with evidence: 1 YES (nav and hero name GitBox), 2 YES (the Kanban preview in the hero), 3 was NO → YES for the changed card after the fix (the title says what the user gets), 4 YES, 5 NO (static benefit blocks in card shells, pre-existing), 6 NO (hover lift on blocks that do nothing, pre-existing), 7 NO (pre-existing).
- 4A. Title "Move Between Boards" names a command (both voices). Native proposes "Set Work Aside"; Codex "Keep Active Work in Focus". **Decided: "Set Work Aside"**, icon `ArrowRightLeft`, body as written in the plan. It is short like its three neighbours ("Kanban Board", "Drag & Drop", "Project Info") and says the outcome. `LayoutGrid` was dropped because it reads like `Columns3` on the first card; `Archive` because nothing is an archive any more. Taste decision T3 (updates the CEO's draft).
- 4B. Static blocks carry `cursor-pointer` (Codex). **Fixed:** removed; they are not links. The hover lift and shadow stay: restyling the section is a redesign this issue does not ask for. Taste decision T6.
- 4C. Found while reading the file: the "Project Info" card promises "secure credentials", a feature removed in December 2025. **Fixed:** the sentence is corrected. A landing page that promises something the app does not have costs trust.
- App UI: no new card, no new chrome. The create form in the dialog is a label, a field and a button.
- Remaining gap to 10: the 2×2 feature grid itself is a generic pattern (litmus 5 to 7). Out of scope.

#### Pass 5: Design system alignment — 6/10 → 8/10

No DESIGN.md; rated on what the plan specifies. Gap recorded; `/design-consultation` is the way to get one, not this PR.

- No new component. The create form uses `Label`, `Input`, `Button`; the retry is a `Button variant="outline"`; the two links use the treatment of `HeldRepositoryList.tsx:78`; the error under the field uses the dialog's existing inline alert pattern with `role="alert"`.
- The `blank` preset lives next to the six presets in `board-presets.ts` and is not in the list the selector renders.
- Icons come from `lucide-react`, already the only icon set.
- Remaining gap to 10: tokens are not named because there is no DESIGN.md to name them from.

#### Pass 6: Responsive & accessibility — 3/10 → 8/10

- 6A. `/account`: "two tiles in a two-column grid" did not say at which widths (Codex wants one column below `sm`). **Decided: `grid-cols-2` at every width**, like the three tiles today; two tiles of a 320 px screen are about 130 px wide, and the label "Repository Cards" may wrap. The local check opens the page at 320 px. Taste decision T7.
- 6B. Focus after the card leaves the board (Codex). Today focus falls to the page after Move or Remove; this plan does not make it worse and does not fix it. **Decided: TODO** "Move focus when a card leaves the board" (new, design debt), because it needs card refs across columns and touches Remove and Undo too.
- 6C. Focus and announcements inside the new form. **Fixed:** visible label, Enter submits, the button is disabled and reads "Creating…", the error has `role="alert"`, focus goes to Move after creation. Toasts are announced by `sonner`.
- 6D. Touch targets: the new button and links use the existing sizes of the dialog (the link treatment already has the larger touch size). No new exception.
- Keyboard: the local check walks card menu → move dialog → pick a board → Move with the keyboard only.
- Sidebar collapsed state: checked on a screenshot.
- Remaining gap to 10: 6B.

#### Pass 7: Unresolved design decisions

| Decision needed                                     | Decided                                          | If it had been deferred                                        |
| --------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------ | -------------------------------------------------------------- |
| One-board user: link or create in place             | create in the dialog, `blank` preset             | the user creates a board and loses the card they came for      |
| Sidebar after removal                               | one divider                                      | an empty band between two rules, also in the README screenshot |
| Remove confirmation                                 | names the safe alternative                       | notes deleted by users who wanted to archive                   |
| Move success                                        | toast names the board, action opens it           | "moved… where?"                                                |
| Fetch failure, column-less board                    | retry; link to the board                         | two dead ends in the only archive path                         |
| Column name on the migrated board                   | "Repositories" (Taste T8)                        | "Archived" suggests a special state                            |
| Landing fourth card                                 | "Set Work Aside", `ArrowRightLeft`               | a command name with an icon that repeats the first card        |
| `/account` grid                                     | two tiles, `grid-cols-2`, loading state matches  | three skeletons collapsing into two tiles                      |
| Favorite flag, top insertion, undo, no preselection | not done (T4, T5, and the plan's "Out of scope") | —                                                              |
| Focus after a card leaves                           | TODO                                             | stays as today                                                 |

9 resolved, 1 deferred (focus after a card leaves the board, TODO).

#### Required outputs (design)

**NOT in scope:** favorite flag on the migrated board; a link to it in the sidebar; an in-app notice; top insertion for moved cards; undo for a move; a dialog that starts without a selection; a redesign of the landing feature grid (cards, hover motion); a seventh preset card on `/boards/new`; a DESIGN.md.

**What already exists:** listed in Step 0. Nothing new is built except one form inside an existing dialog.

**TODOS.md:** one new entry, "Move focus when a card leaves the board". What: after Move to Another Board or Remove from Board, put focus on the next card of the column, or on the column's Add button when it is empty. Why: the focused element disappears and keyboard and screen reader users are dropped at the top of the page. Pros: the two most used card actions stop losing the user's place. Cons: needs a way to address cards across columns from the dialog and the confirmation; Undo has to restore focus too. Context: `MoveToAnotherBoardDialog`, `OverflowMenu`, `useOptimisticCardAction`. Depends on: nothing. Decision: A, add to TODOS.md (auto; C "build it now" is above the threshold, it touches the undo path).

**Approved mockups:** none generated.

Messages carry counts only.\n\nA second run after success does nothing: the block starts with `IF to_regclass('public.maintenance') IS NULL THEN RETURN`. A failed run changes nothing (one transaction), so there is no partial state to resume from.\n\nSteps, in this order:\n\n1. Lock `maintenance`, `projectinfo` and `repocard` in `ACCESS EXCLUSIVE` mode.\n2. Guard: count the entries whose repository is already on one of the user's boards (compared with `lower()`, the key of `repocard_unique_repo_per_user`) and the groups of entries of one user that differ only in letter case. When either count is above 0, raise an exception that carries the two counts and change nothing. No entry and no note is ever dropped by this migration.\n3. For each user with entries, in this order (the card triggers `set_repocard_user_id` and `check_repocard_status_board` read the board and the column):\n   - Board: name `Maintenance`, `position = COALESCE(MAX(position), -1) + 1` over the user's boards. A user who already has a board with that name still gets a new one; board names are not unique.\n   - Column: name `Archived`, default color, `order` 0, grid 0/0.\n   - Cards: one per entry. The card reuses the entry's `id`, keeps `created_at`/`updated_at`, gets `meta = '{}'` (what `restore_to_board` wrote) and `order` 0..n by `updated_at DESC NULLS LAST, id` (the order the Maintenance page showed).\n4. Re-point `projectinfo`: `SET repo_card_id = maintenance_id, maintenance_id = NULL` for every row with a `maintenance_id`, in one statement (the exclusive CHECK holds throughout). The trigger `update_projectinfo_updated_at` is disabled immediately before this statement and enabled again immediately after it, so `updated_at` is not rewritten.\n5. Drop `move_to_maintenance(uuid, uuid, text, text)` and `restore_to_board(uuid, uuid, uuid, text, text, integer)` with `IF EXISTS`.\n6. `projectinfo`: drop every policy on the table by looping over `pg_policies` (the migrations define two, \"Users can view their project info\" and \"Users can manage their project info\"; a count other than 2 is reported as a `WARNING`), drop `projectinfo_target_exclusive_check`, drop column `maintenance_id` (takes `projectinfo_maintenance_id_key`, the FK and `idx_projectinfo_maintenance_id` with it), set `repo_card_id NOT NULL`, recreate the two policies with the repocard → board path only (same names, same `(select auth.uid())` form as `20260208_reenable_rls_optimized_policies.sql`; the `FOR ALL` policy keeps a `WITH CHECK` equal to its `USING`).\n7. `DROP TABLE maintenance` (its policies, trigger and index go with it). The table is not kept under another name: the dump of section 6, step 5 is the copy.\n8. `RAISE NOTICE` with three counts: entries moved, boards created, `projectinfo` rows re-pointed. The table is gone after this, so the message in the job log is the record of what was moved.\n\nBecause the card reuses the entry's `id`, a note draft the browser saved under that id (the persisted `draft` slice) continues on the card.\n\nType files: edit `src/lib/supabase/types.ts` by hand (it carries hand-written aliases; remove the `maintenance` table, `projectinfo.maintenance_id`, the two functions and the `Maintenance` alias; `repo_card_id` becomes `string`). Delete `src/lib/supabase/database.types.ts`: `src/app/maintenance/page.tsx` is its only importer. Remove its entries from `knip.config.ts` and `eslint.config.mjs`.\n\nA user whose only data was Maintenance entries ends up with \"Maintenance\" as their only board, so the first-board creation on sign-in does not run for them. Accepted.\n\nRehearsal before the merge, on local Supabase. Every `--linked` command is run only after `supabase/.temp/project-ref` is confirmed to read `zfcwvzazzzsefbkztsyp` (production). The host has no `psql`; SQL goes through `docker exec -i supabase_db_gitbox psql -U postgres`.\n\nScenario A, rows built by hand:\n\n1. `supabase db reset --local --version 20261011030000 --no-seed` (the schema production has today).\n2. Insert: a user with two entries and no board; a user with one board, one card and one entry that has `projectinfo`.\n3. Guard, overlap: add an entry for the repository of the second user's card. `supabase migration up --local` fails with counts 1 and 0; no board was created, the table is still there. Delete that entry.\n4. Guard, letter case: add an entry that differs from an existing one only in letter case. `supabase migration up --local` fails with counts 0 and 1. Delete that entry.\n5. `supabase migration up --local` succeeds. Expect: the first user gets `Maintenance` at position 0 with 2 cards; the second user gets `Maintenance` after their board with 1 card that carries the entry's `projectinfo` with an unchanged `updated_at`; the `NOTICE` reads 3 entries, 2 boards, 1 `projectinfo` row.\n\nScenario B, a copy of production:\n\n6. `supabase db dump --linked --data-only --schema public --file <path>` with the path outside the repository and outside any synced directory. No `auth` data is dumped.\n7. Reset as in step 1, insert one stub `auth.users` row per user id the dump references, load the dump.\n8. `supabase migration up --local`. Check with count queries: 1 new board `Maintenance`, its card count equals the dumped `maintenance` row count, every `projectinfo` row that was on an entry is on the card with the same id and keeps its `updated_at`, `repocard` lost no row, table `maintenance` and column `maintenance_id` are gone, the two policies exist, `update_projectinfo_updated_at` is enabled (`tgenabled = 'O'`). The `NOTICE` reports the same numbers.\n9. Early return: `supabase migration repair --status reverted 20261012090000 --local`, then `supabase migration up --local` again. The block returns at its first statement and the counts of step 8 are unchanged. (Without the repair the CLI skips the file and the early return is never executed.)\n\nThe verification query (rehearsal step 8 and section 6, step 7):\n\n```sql\nSELECT\n  to_regclass('public.maintenance') IS NULL AS table_gone,\n  NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM information_schema.columns\n    WHERE table_schema = 'public' AND table_name = 'projectinfo'\n      AND column_name = 'maintenance_id') AS column_gone,\n  (SELECT count(*) FROM board WHERE name = 'Maintenance') AS boards_named_maintenance,\n  (SELECT count(*) FROM repocard r JOIN board b ON b.id = r.board_id\n    WHERE b.name = 'Maintenance') AS cards_on_them,\n  (SELECT count(*) FROM projectinfo p JOIN repocard r ON r.id = p.repo_card_id\n    JOIN board b ON b.id = r.board_id WHERE b.name = 'Maintenance') AS projectinfo_on_them,\n  (SELECT count(*) FROM pg_policies\n    WHERE schemaname = 'public' AND tablename = 'projectinfo') AS projectinfo_policies,\n  (SELECT tgenabled FROM pg_trigger WHERE tgname = 'update_projectinfo_updated_at'\n    AND tgrelid = 'public.projectinfo'::regclass) AS projectinfo_trigger;\n```\n\nThe database of scenario B stays in place for the local check and the screenshots (section 6, step 3). `pnpm db:reset` puts the local database back to the seed afterwards.\n\n","newText":"### 1. Migration `20261012090000_remove_maintenance_mode.sql`\n\nThe timestamp sorts after `20261011030000`, the newest migration. One `DO` block (atomic however the CLI wraps the file), `lock_timeout` 5s. Messages carry counts only.\n\nA second run after success does nothing: the block starts with `IF to_regclass('public.maintenance') IS NULL THEN RETURN`. A failed run changes nothing (one transaction), so there is no partial state to resume from.\n\nSteps, in this order:\n\n1. Lock `maintenance`, `projectinfo` and `repocard` in `ACCESS EXCLUSIVE` mode.\n2. Guard: count the entries whose repository is already on one of the user's boards (compared with `lower()`, the key of `repocard_unique_repo_per_user`) and the groups of entries of one user that differ only in letter case. When either count is above 0, raise an exception that carries the two counts and change nothing. No entry and no note is ever dropped by this migration.\n3. For each user with entries, in this order (the card triggers `set_repocard_user_id` and `check_repocard_status_board` read the board and the column):\n   - Board: name `Maintenance`, `position = COALESCE(MAX(position), -1) + 1` over the user's boards. A user who already has a board with that name still gets a new one; board names are not unique.\n   - Column: name `Repositories`, default color, `order` 0, grid 0/0. The name is neutral on purpose: \"Archived\" inside a board named \"Maintenance\" would suggest a second special state.\n   - Cards: one per entry. The card reuses the entry's `id`, keeps `created_at`/`updated_at`, gets `meta = '{}'` (what `restore_to_board` wrote) and `order` 0..n by `updated_at DESC NULLS LAST, id` (the order the Maintenance page showed).\n4. Re-point `projectinfo`: `SET repo_card_id = maintenance_id, maintenance_id = NULL` for every row with a `maintenance_id`, in one statement (the exclusive CHECK holds throughout). The trigger `update_projectinfo_updated_at` is disabled immediately before this statement and enabled again immediately after it, so `updated_at` is not rewritten.\n5. Drop `move_to_maintenance(uuid, uuid, text, text)` and `restore_to_board(uuid, uuid, uuid, text, text, integer)` with `IF EXISTS`.\n6. `projectinfo`: drop every policy on the table by looping over `pg_policies` (the migrations define two, \"Users can view their project info\" and \"Users can manage their project info\"; a count other than 2 is reported as a `WARNING`), drop `projectinfo_target_exclusive_check`, drop column `maintenance_id` (takes `projectinfo_maintenance_id_key`, the FK and `idx_projectinfo_maintenance_id` with it), set `repo_card_id NOT NULL`, recreate the two policies with the repocard → board path only (same names, same `(select auth.uid())` form as `20260208_reenable_rls_optimized_policies.sql`; the `FOR ALL` policy keeps a `WITH CHECK` equal to its `USING`).\n7. `DROP TABLE maintenance` (its policies, trigger and index go with it). The table is not kept under another name: the dump of section 6, step 5 is the copy.\n8. `RAISE NOTICE` with three counts: entries moved, boards created, `projectinfo` rows re-pointed. The table is gone after this, so the message in the job log is the record of what was moved.\n\nBecause the card reuses the entry's `id`, a note draft the browser saved under that id (the persisted `draft` slice) continues on the card.\n\nType files: edit `src/lib/supabase/types.ts` by hand (it carries hand-written aliases; remove the `maintenance` table, `projectinfo.maintenance_id`, the two functions and the `Maintenance` alias; `repo_card_id` becomes `string`). Delete `src/lib/supabase/database.types.ts`: `src/app/maintenance/page.tsx` is its only importer. Remove its entries from `knip.config.ts` and `eslint.config.mjs`.\n\nA user whose only data was Maintenance entries ends up with \"Maintenance\" as their only board, so the first-board creation on sign-in does not run for them. The host has no `psql`; SQL goes through `docker exec -i supabase_db_gitbox psql -U postgres`.\n\nScenario A, rows built by hand:\n\n1. `supabase db reset --local --version 20261011030000 --no-seed` (the schema production has today).\n2. Client\n\n- Move the `BoardOption` type from `RestoreToBoardDialog.tsx` into `MoveToAnotherBoardDialog.tsx`, its remaining user. The move dialog's empty state (\"No other boards available. Create another board first.\") gains a link to `/boards/new`: with Maintenance gone, moving a card to another board is the only way to set a repository aside with its notes, and a user with one board has to be able to get there from the dialog. Then delete `src/app/maintenance/**`, `RestoreToBoardDialog` (+ stories), `DeleteMaintenanceDialog`, `ROUTES.MAINTENANCE`.\n- `OverflowMenu`: remove \"Move to Maintenance\", `context`, `onRestoreToBoard`, `onDelete`, the destructive maintenance item and the generic `TId`; ids are `RepoCardId`.\n- Remove the `onMoveToMaintenance`/`onMaintenance` prop chain and `handleMoveToMaintenance`. `useOptimisticCardAction` keeps its remaining caller, Remove from Board; its docstring says so. This default stands unless the engineering review records a different decision.\n- `brands.ts`: delete `MaintenanceId`, `toMaintenanceId`, `CardIdentifier`. `NoteModal`, `useNoteModalDraft` and `draftSlice` use `RepoCardId` instead of a generic id.\n- `HeldRepositoryList`: a held repository is always on another board; the `location` union collapses to the board fields.\n- `AddRepositoryCombobox`: remove `maintenanceRepoIdentifiers` and the page-load fallback set. When placements are unknown, only repositories on the current board are hidden (the server still refuses duplicates by the unique index).\n- Sidebar: remove the \"Maintenance Mode\" item. Command Palette: remove \"Go to Maintenance\" and the `G M - Go to Maintenance` part of the shortcuts hint.\n- `/account`: two tiles (Boards, Repository Cards) in a two-column grid.\n- Landing `FeaturesSection`: the fourth card keeps the promise the old one made (set finished work aside without losing its history) and says how it works now. Title \"Move Between Boards\", icon `LayoutGrid`, text: park finished or paused projects on a board of their own; notes, links and comments move with the card. The section keeps four cards because its grid is two columns wide; three would leave a hole. The design review settles the final wording.\n- `/maintenance` returns the app's 404 page. No redirect: nothing links to it and the product has 2 users.\n\n","newText":"### 3. Client\n\nRemoval:\n\n- Move the `BoardOption` type from `RestoreToBoardDialog.tsx` into `MoveToAnotherBoardDialog.tsx`, its remaining user. Then delete `src/app/maintenance/**`, `RestoreToBoardDialog` (+ stories), `DeleteMaintenanceDialog`, `ROUTES.MAINTENANCE`.\n- `OverflowMenu`: remove \"Move to Maintenance\", `context`, `onRestoreToBoard`, `onDelete`, the destructive maintenance item and the generic `TId`; ids are `RepoCardId`.\n- Remove the `onMoveToMaintenance`/`onMaintenance` prop chain and `handleMoveToMaintenance`. `useOptimisticCardAction` keeps its remaining caller, Remove from Board; its docstring says so. This default stands unless the engineering review records a different decision.\n- `brands.ts`: delete `MaintenanceId`, `toMaintenanceId`, `CardIdentifier`. `NoteModal`, `useNoteModalDraft` and `draftSlice` use `RepoCardId` instead of a generic id.\n- `HeldRepositoryList`: a held repository is always on another board; the `location` union collapses to the board fields.\n- `AddRepositoryCombobox`: remove `maintenanceRepoIdentifiers` and the page-load fallback set. When placements are unknown, only repositories on the current board are hidden (the server still refuses duplicates by the unique index, with the message that names the holding board; that copy is unchanged).\n- Command Palette: remove \"Go to Maintenance\" and the `G M - Go to Maintenance` part of the shortcuts hint.\n- `/maintenance` returns the app's 404 page. No redirect: nothing links to it and the product has 2 users.\n\nSidebar:\n\n- Remove the \"Maintenance Mode\" item and one of the two dividers that framed it. Order after the change, expanded and collapsed: All Boards, Favorites, one divider, Settings, Shortcuts, theme. No link to the migrated board takes the item's place; it is an ordinary board.\n\nMove to Another Board (`MoveToAnotherBoardDialog`), now the only way to set a repository aside with its notes:\n\n| State | What the user sees |\n|---|---|\n| Loading boards | unchanged |\n| Loading fails | the error text plus a \"Try again\" button that loads again; never the \"no other board\" state |\n| No other board | \"You have no other board yet. Create one to move this repository to.\" A labeled field \"Board name\" and a \"Create board\" button. Creating makes a board with one column \"Repositories\" (a `blank` preset in `board-presets.ts` that `createBoard` accepts and the six-card selector on `/boards/new` does not list), loads the boards again, and selects the new board and its column. The user then presses Move. A failed creation shows its error under the field (`role=\"alert\"`) and keeps the typed name |\n| Destination has no column | the existing message plus a link \"Open <board>\" to add one there |\n| Move fails | unchanged: inline alert, selections kept |\n| Move succeeds | toast \"Moved to <board>\" with an \"Open board\" action that goes to that board |\n\n- Focus: after a board is created, focus goes to the Move button. The field has a visible label, the button reads \"Creating…\" and is disabled while the request runs, Enter in the field submits.\n- The dialog keeps preselecting the first board when there are several.\n\nRemove from Board (confirmation in `OverflowMenu`):\n\n- The text that says the note, links and comment are deleted gains one sentence: \"To keep them, use Move to Another Board instead.\" Separator and destructive styling stay.\n\n`/account`:\n\n- Two tiles in this order: Boards, Repository Cards. `grid-cols-2` at every width (the three tiles were `grid-cols-3` at every width). `account/loading.tsx` shows two skeleton tiles in the same grid, so the page does not jump from three to two.\n\nLanding `FeaturesSection`:\n\n- Fourth card: icon `ArrowRightLeft`, title \"Set Work Aside\", text \"Move finished or paused repositories to a board of their own. Notes, links and comments stay with each card, so nothing is lost when you bring it back.\" The card keeps the promise the old one made and says how it works now. Four cards stay: the grid is two columns wide from `md` up.\n- The \"Project Info\" card stops promising \"secure credentials\"; that feature was removed in `20251231000000_remove_credential_auditlog.sql`. New text: \"Add notes and important links to each repository. Keep all project context in one place.\"\n- The four cards lose `cursor-pointer`: they are not links.\n\n"},{"oldText":"### 4. Tests and fixtures\n\n- Delete the 4 `maintenance-*.spec.ts` files and `MaintenanceClient.test.tsx`.\n- Remove maintenance cases from the other e2e specs and unit tests; keep every case that tests board behavior. `one-repo-across-boards.spec.ts` and the placement unit tests keep the cross-board cases. A case that used a Maintenance entry only as \"some repository held elsewhere\" uses a card on another board instead.\n- `command-palette.spec.ts` uses \"Go to Maintenance\" as the unrelated command that a search for \"boards\" must filter out. It switches to \"Go to Settings\", so the assertion can still fail.\n- `ssr-hydration.spec.ts` lists the landing feature headings; its 'Maintenance Mode' entry becomes the new card's title, so the new card is asserted. `server-rendered-client-components.test.ts` drops its `MaintenanceClient` entry.\n- `seed.sql`: drop maintenance rows, their `projectinfo`, the `maintenance_id` column from inserts and the header lines that list them. `db-query.ts`: drop maintenance helpers. `mocks/handlers`: drop maintenance handlers and data. Stories: drop maintenance variants.\n- New tests, each for behavior that exists after the change. No test asserts that a removed thing is absent: nothing could bring it back by accident, and such a test would itself contain the word the grep forbids.\n  - Unit: the move dialog with no other board shows the link to `/boards/new`; `/account` shows the Boards and Repository Cards tiles; the placement split and the 23505 mapping with board placements only.\n  - E2E: a card moved to another board and back keeps its note (the park and bring back path); the picker lists a repository of another board under \"Already placed elsewhere\".\n  - Migration: no committed test. The rehearsal in section 1 is the check; its counts are recorded in the PR.\n\n","newText":"### 4. Tests and fixtures\n\n- Delete the 4 `maintenance-*.spec.ts` files and `MaintenanceClient.test.tsx`.\n- Remove maintenance cases from the other e2e specs and unit tests; keep every case that tests board behavior. `one-repo-across-boards.spec.ts` and the placement unit tests keep the cross-board cases. A case that used a Maintenance entry only as \"some repository held elsewhere\" uses a card on another board instead.\n- `command-palette.spec.ts` uses \"Go to Maintenance\" as the unrelated command that a search for \"boards\" must filter out. It switches to \"Go to Settings\", so the assertion can still fail.\n- `ssr-hydration.spec.ts` lists the landing feature headings; its 'Maintenance Mode' entry becomes the new card's title, so the new card is asserted. `server-rendered-client-components.test.ts` drops its `MaintenanceClient` entry.\n- `seed.sql`: drop maintenance rows, their `projectinfo`, the `maintenance_id` column from inserts and the header lines that list them. `db-query.ts`: drop maintenance helpers. `mocks/handlers`: drop maintenance handlers and data. Stories: drop maintenance variants.\n- New tests, each for behavior that exists after the change. No test asserts that a removed thing is absent: nothing could bring it back by accident, and such a test would itself contain the word the grep forbids.\n  - Unit, move dialog: with no other board the create form is shown, a created board becomes the selected destination and Move is enabled; a failed load shows \"Try again\" and loads again; a destination without a column links to that board; a successful move shows the toast with its \"Open board\" action.\n  - Unit: `createBoard` with the `blank` preset creates one column named \"Repositories\"; the preset selector still lists six presets; the Remove from Board confirmation names Move to Another Board; `/account` shows the Boards and Repository Cards tiles and its loading state shows two; the placement split and the 23505 mapping with board placements only.\n  - E2E: a card moved to another board and back keeps its note (the park and bring back path); the picker lists a repository of another board under \"Already placed elsewhere\".\n  - Migration: no committed test. The rehearsal in section 1 is the check; its counts are recorded in the PR.\n\n"},{"oldText":"### 5. Docs\n\n- `SPEC.md`: remove the Maintenance sections, routes, data model and wireframes; describe archiving as \"a board of your own\".\n- `README.md`, `CLAUDE.md` (schema is 6 tables, route tree, verification rules without Maintenance, the fixture-auth steps that open `/maintenance`, server action list, board data paragraph), `TODOS.md` (delete \"Enforce board vs Maintenance exclusivity in the database\"; reword the entries that mention Maintenance, restore or the restore dialog; add \"Refresh a card's GitHub metadata\": `meta` is a snapshot from the moment a card is added and nothing updates it).\n- Retake `public/screenshots/app-screenshot.png` (1200×670, README) and `public/screenshots/social-preview.png` (1280×640). Both show the owner's \"Laststance\" board in the dark theme with \"Maintenance Mode\" in the sidebar. Same board and theme, taken from the local copy of production; only public repositories in the frame.\n- `CHANGELOG.md` and version through `/ship`.\n\n","newText":"### 5. Docs\n\n- `SPEC.md`: remove the Maintenance sections, routes, data model and wireframes; describe archiving as \"a board of your own\".\n- `README.md`, `CLAUDE.md` (schema is 6 tables, route tree, verification rules without Maintenance, the fixture-auth steps that open `/maintenance`, server action list, board data paragraph), `TODOS.md` (delete \"Enforce board vs Maintenance exclusivity in the database\"; reword the entries that mention Maintenance, restore or the restore dialog; add \"Refresh a card's GitHub metadata\": `meta` is a snapshot from the moment a card is added and nothing updates it; add \"Move focus when a card leaves the board\": after Move to Another Board or Remove from Board the focused card is gone and focus falls to the page).\n- Retake `public/screenshots/app-screenshot.png` (1200×670, README) and `public/screenshots/social-preview.png` (1280×640). Both show the owner's \"Laststance\" board in the dark theme with \"Maintenance Mode\" in the sidebar. Same board and theme, taken from the local copy of production running a production build (`pnpm build` and `pnpm start` with the local Supabase URL and anon key, so no dev indicator is in the frame), after the sidebar change; only public repositories in the frame.\n- `CHANGELOG.md` and version through `/ship`.\n\n"},{"oldText":"### 6. Rollout\n\n1. `pnpm test`, `pnpm lint`, `pnpm build`, `pnpm typecheck`, `pnpm e2e:parallel` (the E2E run resets the local database, so it comes first).\n2. Rehearse the migration (section 1, scenarios A and B).\n3. Local check as `ryota-murakami` on `http://localhost:3008`, on the database of scenario B. Sign in first (local GoTrue gives the user a new id), then re-point the copied rows from the stub owner id to the local id, in this order: `board.user_id`, then `repocard.user_id` (its trigger copies the owner from the board and fires only when the card row itself is updated), then `user_settings` and `user_link_presets` (delete the local user's own row first where the key would collide). Check: the migrated `Maintenance` board and its notes, Move to Another Board in both directions, Remove from Board, the picker's \"Already placed elsewhere\" for a repository on the `Maintenance` board, repositories added from the picker to a new board (two columns, one private or organization repository), sidebar, palette, `/account`, `/maintenance`. Then the two screenshots of section 5; before they are committed, each image is looked at once more for a private repository name.\n4. `/review`, `/design-review`, `/ship` (draft PR first; CI runs on PRs only). One PR, three commits that each pass `pnpm typecheck`: (a) server and client removal with their tests, the move dialog link and the landing card; (b) the migration, `types.ts`, the seed and the fixtures; (c) docs and screenshots. The migration is reviewed as its own commit.\n5. Right before the merge: run the audit query on production again and note the `entries` count. Expected `entries_also_on_a_board` and `case_variant_groups`: 0. Nothing writes to Maintenance between this step and the migration run: the owner is the only user with entries and is away, and no check uses Move to Maintenance or Restore on production. Take a data-only dump of production's `public` schema to a local file outside the repository. Confirm that the owner has at least two repositories on no board (the scratch boards of step 9 need them). Wait for the Vercel production deployment of the merge commit.\n9. On two scratch boards: add repositories, Move to Another Board between them, Remove from Board; delete both boards afterwards. <!-- autoplan-accepted:design -->

- Sidebar: exactly one divider stands between the board navigation and Settings, in the expanded and in the collapsed state. Checked on a screenshot of each.
- Move dialog: each row of the dialog's state table (the Client part of this plan) is implemented as written, with its exact copy. Every row except "Loading boards" and "Move fails" has a unit test.
- The `blank` preset is accepted by `createBoard` and is not rendered by the selector on `/boards/new`, which keeps six cards. Both facts have a unit test.
- Local check, in addition to section 6, step 3: one pass with the keyboard only through card menu, move dialog and Move; one pass at a phone-sized viewport on a card near the end of the `Maintenance` column (scroll to it, open its note, move it to a working board and back); `/account` at 320 px. The results go into the PR body.
- The two screenshots are taken from a production build at exactly 1200×670 and 1280×640, after the sidebar change.
- The final report to the owner contains the direct link to the migrated board and this sentence: "Your saved repositories are now on an ordinary board named Maintenance. Move cards between boards to set work aside or bring it back."
- `/design-review` runs on the implemented result before the PR is marked ready, and what it finds is fixed in the same PR.
